### [97] [Quantum critical collapse in the large $D$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20705)
*Chih-Hung Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-标量场临界坍缩中量子自能增长模式在大维度极限下的行为，证明了通用李雅普诺夫指数在连续自相似(CSS)和离散自相似(DSS)时空中均保持存在，并发现量子效应会在大维度下产生量子壁，其半径随$(D-2)^{-2}$缩放；同时构造了在DSS域中保持光滑的协变单圈作用量，揭示了回声对称性在控制真空极化中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析可解的爱因斯坦-标量场临界坍缩模型中，量子自能以李雅普诺夫指数$D-2$增长，最终与控制黑洞形成的经典不稳定性竞争。这种竞争会移动裸奇点阈值并在半经典控制下产生有限质量间隙，但当时空维度$D\to\infty$时，这种半经典控制的有效性受到威胁。因此需要系统研究量子增长模式在大维度极限下的行为，以验证半经典描述的可靠性边界。

Method: 1. 在连续自相似(CSS)和离散自相似(DSS)临界时空中分别研究大维度极限下的量子增长模式；2. 在CSS情况下，通过将大$D$极限的视界追踪问题约化为贝塞尔问题，分析量子壁的形成及其半径标度关系；3. 在DSS情况下，研究回声调制对量子增长的影响和记忆效应；4. 构造协变的单圈作用量，其应力张量在整个DSS域中保持光滑，并将其组织成无记忆的回声周期结构；5. 分析普朗克尺度层级在有限维度下的维持条件。

Result: 1. 通用李雅普诺夫指数在CSS和DSS两种自相似形式下均得以保持；2. 在CSS中，经典的裸奇点演化被困陷面的出现所中断；3. 在大$D$极限下，量子壁半径遵循标度关系$r_h\propto(D-2)^{-2}$；4. 该半径在有限$D$时可以保持在普朗克尺度之上，但随着$D$增大，维持这种层级关系变得越来越困难；5. 在DSS中，量子增长受到回声调制，并可能保留先前回声的记忆；6. 最简单的反常诱导作用量表现出累积记忆但在球心处奇异；7. 构造的协变单圈作用量在DSS域中保持光滑，可组织成无记忆的回声周期结构。

Conclusion: 研究确认了量子自能增长模式的通用李雅普诺夫指数在大维度极限下的稳健性，揭示了量子效应通过形成量子壁中断经典裸奇点演化的机制。尽管在大$D$时维持普朗克尺度以上的半径层级变得困难，但半经典描述在有限维度仍然可控。更重要的是，通过构造光滑的协变单圈作用量并将其组织成回声周期结构，研究表明真实四维DSS Choptuik时空的光滑真空极化可能同样受其精确回声对称性支配，这为理解临界坍缩中的量子效应提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: In analytically tractable models of Einstein-scalar critical collapse, it has been shown that the quantum self-energy of the collapsing matter in the dominant $s$-wave sector grows with Lyapunov exponent $D-2$, eventually competing with the classical instability that controls black hole formation. This competition shifts the naked singularity threshold and produces a finite mass gap within semiclassical control, yet that control may appear threatened as the spacetime dimension $D\to\infty$. We study this quantum growing mode in both continuously (CSS) and discretely (DSS) self-similar critical spacetimes at large $D$ and establish that the universal Lyapunov exponent survives both forms of self-similarity. In CSS, the classical approach to a naked singularity is interrupted by the appearance of trapped surfaces. At large $D$, the horizon tracing reduces to a Bessel problem, yielding a quantum wall whose radius obeys $r_h\propto(D-2)^{-2}$. This radius can remain above the Planck scale at finite $D$, although maintaining this hierarchy becomes increasingly demanding as $D$ grows. In DSS, the same growth is modulated by echoing and can retain memory of previous echoes. The simplest anomaly-induced action exhibits accumulating memory but is singular at the spherical center. We construct a covariant one-loop action whose stress tensor remains smooth throughout the DSS domain and organize it into an echo-periodic structure without memory. This suggests that the smooth vacuum polarization of the genuine four-dimensional DSS Choptuik spacetime may similarly be governed by its exact echoing symmetry.

</details>


### [98] [Towards ultrarelativistic scattering near the light ring: asymptotic divergence of gravitational-wave fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20708)
*David Trestini,Riccardo Gonzo,Benjamin Leather*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超相对论性粒子在史瓦西黑洞光环附近散射时产生的引力波辐射通量特性,利用WKB近似方法推导出通量发散的精确对数增强系数,并揭示了该系数在辐射到无穷远和被视界吸收两种情况下完全相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当粒子在散射与被黑洞捕获的临界状态附近运动时,会在不稳定圆轨道(超相对论极限下接近光环)附近停留很长时间。这一物理场景下的引力波辐射通量行为尚未被充分理解,特别是其发散特性和对数增强机制需要精确的理论描述,这对于理解自力效应观测量的渐近行为具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin(WKB)近似方法求解Regge-Wheeler方程,解析处理无穷调和级数求和,推导通量发散的精确表达式。结合低多极矩的数值计算和大多极矩的精确渐近分析,估算次主导极点贡献的系数。研究在质量比一阶微扰框架下进行,并将结果推广至Kerr时空中的赤道圆轨道情况。

Result: 证明了引力波通量以与光环距离的倒数乘以其对数的形式发散,并推导出对数增强系数的精确表达式。发现辐射到无穷远和被视界吸收的通量具有完全相同的对数增强系数。成功估算了次主导极点贡献系数,并对Kerr时空情况提出了推广猜想。研究结果还揭示了光环附近超相对论散射轨道能量和角动量损失的解析结构。

Conclusion: WKB方法能够有效提供自力效应观测量在数值模式求和变得困难的参数区域中的渐近行为。本研究为理解黑洞光环附近超相对论粒子的引力波辐射特性提供了精确的理论框架,特别是对数发散系数的普适性(在两种通量中相同)揭示了该物理过程的深层对称性,为后续研究Kerr黑洞和更复杂引力系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Near the threshold between scattering and capture by a Schwarzschild black hole, a particle spends a long time close to an unstable circular orbit, which in the ultrarelativistic limit approaches the light ring. Here we study the behavior of the gravitational wave fluxes emitted by such an ultrarelativistic particle at first order in the mass ratio. The fluxes radiated to infinity and absorbed by the horizon diverge as the inverse distance from the light ring multiplied by its logarithm. Using the Wentzel, Kramers, and Brillouin (WKB) approximation to solve the Regge-Wheeler equation and treating the infinite harmonic sum analytically, we derive an exact expression for the coefficient of this logarithmic enhancement and show that it is identical for both fluxes. We then estimate the coefficients of the subleading pole contributions by combining numerical calculations at low multipoles with the exact asymptotics at large multipoles. We further conjecture an extension to equatorial circular orbits in Kerr. Interestingly, our results also inform the analytical structure of the energy and angular momentum losses of ultrarelativistic scattering orbits near the light ring. More broadly, our work illustrates how WKB methods can provide the asymptotic behavior of self-force observables in regimes where numerical mode sums become increasingly challenging.

</details>


### [99] [Optimal Strategies for Gravitational-Wave Memory Detection with Noise Marginalization in Pulsar Timing Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20772)
*Rand Burnette,Xavier Siemens*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将引力波记忆的最优频率论统计方法整合到enterprise代码套件中,通过模拟脉冲星计时阵列数据和NANOGrav 15年数据集,比较了噪声边缘化方法与最大后验方法的有效性,发现两种方法在检测统计上差异很小,最大后验方法足以识别潜在可探测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脉冲星计时阵列可以探测引力波记忆效应,这将为广义相对论提供独特的检验机会。为了优化在脉冲星计时阵列中搜索引力波记忆的方法,需要将最优统计量集成到现有分析工具中,并评估不同统计方法的效能。

Method: 1. 将引力波记忆的最优频率论统计量整合到enterprise代码套件中;2. 测试该统计量的噪声边缘化版本,并与更简单的最大后验方法进行比较;3. 在包含红噪声和白噪声的模拟脉冲星计时阵列数据上进行测试;4. 在NANOGrav 15年真实观测数据集上进行验证分析。

Result: 在模拟数据集上,噪声边缘化方法和最大后验方法之间的检测统计量没有显著差异;在NANOGrav 15年数据的比较中仅发现微小差异。两种方法在现实脉冲星计时阵列数据分析中表现相近。

Conclusion: 对于实际的脉冲星计时阵列,噪声边缘化方法可用于为贝叶斯结果分配虚警概率,但最大后验方法已足够用于识别潜在可探测的引力波记忆信号。最大后验方法因其简单性和有效性,可作为引力波记忆搜索的首选方法。

Abstract: Current pulsar timing arrays could detect gravitational wave memory, which would provide a unique test of general relativity. In order to optimize our searches for gravitational wave memory in pulsar timing arrays, we integrate the optimal frequentist statistics for GW memory into the \texttt{enterprise} code suite. We additionally test a noise marginalized version of this statistic, and compare it's effectiveness to the simpler maximum posterior method. We perform these tests on simulated pulsar timing array with red and white noise content, as well as the NANOGrav $15$-year data set. We find no significant variations in our detection statistics between the noise-marginalized and the maximum-posterior methods for the simulated datasets, and only small variations in the NANOGrav $15$-year comparisons. We conclude that for realistic PTAs the noise-marginalization method could be useful for assigning a false alarm probability to Bayesian results, though the maximum posterior method is sufficient for identifying potentially detectable signals.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum-Corrected and Hairy Black Holes via Analogue Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20996)
*Christian Noberto de Souza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将两种奇异黑洞几何映射到拉瓦尔喷嘴的跨声速流动,并用气动变量解读其微扰响应。研究了引力解耦的毛黑洞和Calmet-Kuipers度规的准简谐模频谱,发现量子修正主要影响标量和狄拉克通道(质量因子变化达37%),而毛发对所有通道影响相同;通过龟坐标等价性将势垒问题转化为Sturm-Liouville问题,确定了流动剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黑洞微扰研究主要集中在标准时空几何上,而对修正引力理论和量子效应导致的奇异黑洞几何的动力学响应缺乏直观理解。通过将黑洞几何映射到经典流体力学中的de Laval喷嘴跨声速流动,可以用熟悉的气动变量来解读黑洞的微扰行为,为理解引力解耦毛发和量子修正对黑洞准简谐模的影响提供新的物理图像。

Method: 研究采用以下方法:(1)选取两种修正黑洞度规:受主导能量条件约束的引力解耦毛黑洞,以及由六维算符Wilson系数控制的Calmet-Kuipers量子修正度规;(2)将自旋0、1/2、1、2的微扰方程化为薛定谔型波动方程;(3)使用Mashhoon方法以闭合形式求解完整的泛音塔频谱;(4)通过等同两种龟坐标将势垒等价性转化为Sturm-Liouville问题;(5)由给定的势垒确定喷嘴壁面剖面、流动变量和推力系数,建立黑洞几何与喷嘴流动的运动学类比。

Result: 研究获得以下结果:(1)所有准简谐模频率均受阻尼;(2)量子修正使标量和狄拉克通道的质量因子变化高达37%,而电磁和引力通道约为10%;毛发对所有通道的影响基本一致;(3)毛发效应贯穿整个扩张段,而量子修正仅在喉部附近显著,且随质量的四次方反比增长,将其限制在原初黑洞范围;(4)该类比是运动学性质的,喷嘴流动能复现这些几何的微扰散射行为,但不复现其背后的场方程。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了奇异黑洞几何与de Laval喷嘴跨声速流动之间的运动学类比,揭示了引力解耦毛发和量子修正对黑洞准简谐模的不同影响模式:量子效应主要作用于特定通道且局域于喉部附近(仅对原初黑洞显著),而毛发效应对所有通道一视同仁且分布于整个扩张段。这种流体力学类比为理解修正引力理论中的黑洞微扰动力学提供了新的物理直观,但需注意该类比仅在运动学层面成立,不涉及场方程的等价性。

Abstract: This thesis maps two exotic black-hole geometries onto the transonic flow of a de Laval nozzle and reads their perturbative response in aerodynamic variables. The first is the hairy black hole of gravitational decoupling, constrained by the dominant energy condition and by the existence of a horizon. The second is the Calmet-Kuipers metric, short-ranged and governed by the single Wilson coefficient of the dimension-six operator that the two-loop Goroff-Sagnotti divergence identifies. Perturbations of spin 0, 1/2, 1 and 2 reduce to a Schrödinger-type wave equation, whose spectrum the Mashhoon method returns as a whole overtone tower in closed form, every frequency damped. The two corrections are distinguished by which channel responds and by how strongly all of them do, the quantum coupling moving the quality factor by up to 37% in the scalar and Dirac channels and by about 10% in the other two, while the hair acts on every channel alike. Identifying the two tortoise coordinates turns the equality of the potentials into a Sturm-Liouville problem, so that the prescribed barrier fixes the wall profile and with it the flow variables and the thrust coefficient. The hair is felt across the whole divergent section, whereas the quantum correction registers only near the throat and grows as the inverse fourth power of the mass, which confines it to primordial black holes. The analogy is kinematical, so the duct reproduces the scattering of perturbations off these geometries without reproducing the field equations behind them.

</details>


### [101] [Displacement-noise-free gravitational-wave detector using quantum tomography of photon orbital angular momentum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21112)
*Haorong Wu,Xilong Fan,Lixiang Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于光子轨道角动量(OAM)量子跃迁的引力波探测新方案,通过参数化量子态层析技术在OAM希尔伯特空间中提取引力波信息,实现了无位移噪声的宽带探测,灵敏度达到$1.6\times10^{-20}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$,可填补0.1-10 Hz中频引力波探测空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波探测器受位移噪声限制,在0.1-10 Hz中频段存在探测空白。利用引力波诱导光子OAM态量子跃迁这一物理效应,可以开发一种本质上免受位移噪声影响的新型探测方案,并能通过单个V型探测器完整解析引力波的全部七个波参数(频率、传播方向、两个极化振幅及其初始相位)。

Method: 核心方法是利用引力波诱导的光子OAM边带进行参数化量子态层析。引入强度为$r=0.01$的参考态$r|0\rangle_{\mathrm{P}}$来增强探测信号。采用臂长$L=4\,\mathrm{m}$、往返次数$N=160$、傍轴参数$γ=0.1$、入射光功率$P_{\mathrm{BS}}=10\,\mathrm{kW}$、波长$λ_{\mathrm{opt}}=1064\,\mathrm{nm}$的V型探测器配置。通过测量OAM跃迁振幅而非差分臂长来提取引力波信息,边带跃迁振幅按$\propto P_{\mathrm{BS}}ANLγ/λ_{\mathrm{opt}}$比例缩放。

Result: 对于引力波振幅$A=10^{-18}$,估算边带跃迁振幅为$|α_{\pm}|\sim10^{-10}$,信号功率约为$1\,\mathrm{nW}$(直接探测边带信号仅约$10^{-15}\,\mathrm{W}$,引入参考态后显著增强)。散粒噪声限制的灵敏度底限达到$S^{1/2}_n=1.6\times10^{-20}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$。该方案能够解析引力波的全部七个参数,提供宽带探测能力,特别是覆盖0.1-10 Hz中频段。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于OAM量子跃迁的引力波探测框架具有本质上的抗位移噪声特性,通过在OAM希尔伯特空间而非位置空间进行测量,为引力波探测提供了全新的物理途径。该方案能够实现单探测器全参数解析,并有效填补中频引力波探测空白,为未来引力波天文学开辟了新的技术路线,具有重要的科学应用前景。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) induce quantum transitions between photon orbital-angular-momentum (OAM) states with amplitudes $α_{\pm}$. Exploiting this effect, we present a displacement-noise-free detection framework that extracts GW information from the OAM Hilbert space. Parametrized quantum state tomography of the GW-induced OAM sidebands enables a single V-shaped detector to resolve all seven wave parameters: the frequency, propagation direction, two polarization amplitudes, and initial phase of each polarization. Direct detection of the sidebands yields a weak signal of approximately $10^{-15}\,\mathrm{W}$. We therefore introduce a stronger reference state $r |0>_{\mathrm{P}}$, with $r=0.01$, to enhance the detected signal. For an arm length of $L=4\,\mathrm{m}$, $N=160$ round trips, a paraxial parameter of $γ=0.1$, an incident light power of $P_{\mathrm{BS}}=10\,\mathrm{kW}$, a wavelength of $λ_{\mathrm{opt}}=1064\,\mathrm{nm}$, and GW amplitudes of $A=10^{-18}$, we estimate sideband transition amplitudes of $|α_{\pm}|\sim10^{-10}$ and signal powers of order $1\,\mathrm{nW}$, scaling as $P_{\mathrm{BS}}α_{\pm}\propto P_{\mathrm{BS}}ANLγ/λ_{\mathrm{opt}}$. The shot-noise-limited sensitivity floor is $S^{1/2}_n=1.6\times10^{-20}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$. Because the scheme measures OAM transition amplitudes rather than differential arm lengths, it is intrinsically free from displacement noise and provides a broadband probe of GWs, specifically bridging the $0.1$--$10\,\mathrm{Hz}$ mid-frequency gap.

</details>


### [102] [Axial gravitational quasinormal modes of magnetically charged black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21354)
*De-Cheng Zou,Xufen Zhang,Chao-Ming Zhang,Ming Zhang,Rui-Hong Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带磁荷的弦启发Euler-Heisenberg黑洞的轴向引力扰动，利用WKB近似和AIM方法计算准正则模频率和灰体因子，分析了黑洞特征参数对这些物理量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带磁荷的弦启发Euler-Heisenberg黑洞在轴向引力扰动下的动力学稳定性和辐射特性，探讨黑洞特征参数（磁荷$Q_m$、Euler-Heisenberg参数$ε$和多极数$l$）对准正则模频率和灰体因子的影响，这对理解非线性电动力学黑洞的量子性质和霍金辐射具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 将轴向度规扰动与轴向电磁扰动解耦，专注于轴向引力扰动；2) 采用WKB近似方法和渐近迭代法(AIM)计算引力准正则模频率；3) 通过时域演化剖面提取频率结果以验证方法有效性；4) 利用WKB方法计算灰体因子；5) 系统分析磁荷$Q_m$、Euler-Heisenberg参数$ε$和多极数$l$对准正则模频率和灰体因子的影响。

Result: 1) AIM方法得到的准正则模频率与WKB方法的结果高度吻合，包括从时域剖面中提取的结果；2) 成功计算了该黑洞系统的灰体因子；3) 详细揭示了磁荷$Q_m$、Euler-Heisenberg参数$ε$和多极数$l$三个参数对准正则模频率和灰体因子的具体影响规律。

Conclusion: 本文成功分析了带磁荷弦启发Euler-Heisenberg黑洞的轴向引力扰动特性。两种独立数值方法(WKB和AIM)的结果相互验证，确保了计算的可靠性。研究揭示了黑洞特征参数对准正则模频率和灰体因子的影响机制，为理解非线性电动力学框架下黑洞的动力学行为和量子辐射性质提供了重要的理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider axial perturbations on the magnetically charged string-inspired Euler-Heisenberg black hole. Since axial metric perturbation decouples from axial electromagnetic perturbation, here we mainly focus on axial gravitational perturbation. By using WKB approximation and AIM methods, we make a detailed analysis of the gravitational quasinormal frequencies by varying the characteristic parameters of gravitational perturbation and black holes. It is found these results obtained through the AIM method are in good agreement with those of obtained by WKB method, including these results extracted from the time-domain profiles. The greybody factor is calculated by WKB method. The effects of these parameters $Q_m$, $ε$ and multipole number $l$ on the greybody factor are also studied.

</details>


### [103] [Bound States Inside the Horizon and Black Hole Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21426)
*Hassan Firouzjahi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild黑洞内部束缚态微扰，探讨其微观态谱能否提供黑洞熵的统计描述。通过对高激发态引入角动量截断，发现束缚态熵与视界面积成正比（$S_{\rm bs}=αA/\hbar G$），但在引力微扰扇区中，平衡态束缚态对熵和能量的贡献通常很小。若要完全解释黑洞熵，需要黑洞能量的显著份额，此时背景几何的反作用不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞熵的微观起源是理论物理的核心问题之一。本研究旨在通过Schwarzschild黑洞内部束缚态的微观态计数来探索黑洞熵的统计力学描述，检验这些束缚态是否能够解释黑洞的热力学性质，特别是Bekenstein-Hawking熵公式。这为理解黑洞量子性质提供了一个新的视角。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：(1) 分析Schwarzschild黑洞内部束缚态微扰，这些态由量子数$(s, p,\ell,m)$标记，其中$s$为自旋、$p$为径向激发数、$(\ell,m)$为角动量标签；(2) 通过要求高激发态分辨的最短波长保持在量子引力尺度之上，引入角动量截断$\ell_{\rm max}\sim r_H/L_{\rm Pl}$；(3) 在微正则系综中计数相应的微观态配置；(4) 要求束缚态扇区在Hawking温度下与黑洞达到热平衡，以确定束缚态能量占总黑洞能量的比例；(5) 检验束缚态完全解释黑洞熵所需的条件。

Result: 研究得到以下结果：(1) 引入角动量截断后，角简并度与视界面积成正比；(2) 束缚态熵具有形式$S_{\rm bs}=αA/\hbar G$，与黑洞熵的面积律一致；(3) 对于引力微扰扇区，在热平衡条件下，束缚态对熵的贡献和能量占比通常都很小；(4) 在态计数层面，束缚态原则上可以解释完整的黑洞熵，但这需要黑洞能量的order unity份额；(5) 当束缚态能量占比达到显著水平时，对背景几何的反作用效应变得不可忽略。

Conclusion: 本研究表明Schwarzschild黑洞内部束缚态的微观态计数能够产生与视界面积成正比的熵贡献，符合黑洞熵的面积律。然而，在引力微扰扇区且满足热平衡条件下，束缚态对黑洞熵的实际贡献相对较小。虽然态计数层面上束缚态可以完全解释黑洞熵，但这种饱和情况需要束缚态携带黑洞能量的显著份额，此时必须考虑对背景时空几何的反作用效应，这超出了微扰理论的适用范围。因此，束缚态作为黑洞熵微观起源的完整描述仍面临理论一致性挑战。

Abstract: We study the bound states perturbations in the interior of the Schwarzschild black hole and examine whether or not their microstates spectrum can furnish a statistical description of the black hole entropy. These states contain a discrete set of modes characterized by the labels $(s, p,\ell,m)$, where $s$ represents the spin of the species, $p$ denotes the radial excitation number while $(\ell,m)$ are the usual angular momentum labels. Requiring that the shortest wavelength resolved by the highly excited bound states remains above the quantum gravity scale introduces an angular cutoff $\ell_{\rm max}\sim r_H/L_{\rm Pl}$. The resulting angular degeneracy therefore scales with the horizon area. We count the corresponding configurations in the microcanonical ensemble and find an entropy of the form $S_{\rm bs}=αA/\hbar G$. Demanding that the bound state sector to be in thermal equilibrium with the black hole at the Hawking temperature fixes the fraction of the bound state energy in total black hole energy. For the gravitational perturbations sector, the equilibrium bound state contribution in entropy and the energy fraction are typically small. We also examine the conditions under which the bound states could account for the full black hole entropy. Although such saturation is possible at the level of state counting, it requires an order unity fraction in the black hole energy and therefore the backreactions on the background geometry cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Cosmology in Page-Wootters' Framework: Singularity Resolution and Clock Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21528)
*Vishal,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Page-Wootters框架下研究量子宇宙学中大爆炸奇点的命运和不同时钟态之间的相干性。通过正则量子化平面对称Bianchi I宇宙并采用Dirac分解Wheeler-DeWitt约束，构造高斯波包解，发现条件概率密度在零体积极限下消失，表明奇点在概率框架内得到解决；进一步分析约化密度矩阵揭示了量子态的谱性质如何支配时钟态之间的时间相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大爆炸奇点是经典广义相对论中的基本问题，而量子引力理论被期待能够解决这一奇点问题。本文旨在通过Page-Wootters关系量子力学框架，从概率和相干性角度研究量子宇宙学中奇点的解决机制，并探讨不同时钟态之间的量子相干特性如何体现时间的关系性描述。

Method: 采用正则量子化方法处理平面对称的Bianchi I宇宙模型，对Wheeler-DeWitt约束方程进行Dirac分解，得到关于内禀时钟变量的一阶类薛定谔方程。在膨胀分支中构造高斯波包解，由此导出宇宙的条件态和条件概率密度。进一步从不同时钟读数的条件态及剩余自由度构建密度矩阵，通过追踪剩余子系统得到约化密度矩阵，并分析不同时钟态分离程度与相干性的函数关系。

Result: 研究发现条件概率密度在零体积极限（大爆炸奇点处）趋于零，这在概率框架内实现了奇点的解决。约化密度矩阵的分析表明，量子态的谱性质决定了Page-Wootters描述中不同时钟态之间的时间相干性，相干性随时钟态分离程度变化呈现特定的函数关系。

Conclusion: 本文在Page-Wootters框架内提供了大爆炸奇点解决的关系性刻画，证明了量子宇宙学中奇点可以通过条件概率的消失得到解决。研究还揭示了量子态的谱性质与时钟态之间时间相干性的内在联系，为理解量子引力中时间的关系性本质和奇点的量子消解提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the fate of big-bang singularity and coherence between distinct clock states in a quantum cosmological setting within the Page-Wootters framework. With a canonically quantized plane-symmetric Bianchi I universe, we employ Dirac factorization of the Wheeler-DeWitt constraint and obtain a Schrodinger-like equation which is first-order in an internal clock variable. By constructing its solution as a Gaussian wave packet in the expanding branch, we derive the corresponding conditional state and probability density of the universe. We find that the conditional probability density vanishes in the zero-volume limit, indicating resolution of the big-bang singularity within this probabilistic framework. Another prespective of the study is provided by the construction of density matrix from the conditional states associated with different clock readings and the remaining degree of freedom. By tracing out the remaining subsystem, we obtain the reduced density matrix and analyze the resulting coherence as a function of separation between different clock states. These results provide a relational characterization of singularity resolution and demonstrate how the spectral properties of the quantum state govern the temporal coherence between different clock states in the Page-Wootters description.

</details>


### [105] [Numerical simulations of low-mass black hole-neutron star mergers with moderate eccentricities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21560)
*Ivan Markin,Gonzalo Morrás,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟研究了偏心轨道黑洞-中子星系统的引力波信号特征，验证了现有偏心波形模型的有效性，并探讨了强磁化对吸积盘中俘获g模振荡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道偏心率的可靠探测是黑洞-中子星系统动力学形成起源的重要指标。虽然偏心波形模型已经开发出来，但其对黑洞-中子星信号的分析有效性尚未通过数值相对论模拟进行验证。此外，需要评估强磁化环境下吸积盘中俘获g模振荡是否仍然存在。

Method: 基于先前准圆形黑洞-中子星并合研究，对这些系统的子集进行数值相对论模拟，采用的轨道偏心率约为GW200105_162426推断值的五倍。额外进行了磁化物质模拟来评估强磁化盘中的俘获g模振荡。使用有效单体波形模型SEOBNRv5EHM和仅限吸积阶段的后牛顿模型pyEFPE/pyEFPEHM进行对比分析。

Result: SEOBNRv5EHM模型在潮汐贡献不重要时显示出合理的一致性，而pyEFPE/pyEFPEHM模型在晚期吸积阶段表现出较大偏差。对于等质量系统（具有最大有效潮汐形变性），偏心潮汐修正对相位有微小但明显的影响（约0.1弧度）。偏心率对遗迹黑洞性质的影响几乎可忽略，但遗迹盘质量差异可达两倍。吸积盘中俘获g模的频率不随偏心率变化，主要取决于遗迹黑洞的性质。强磁化使吸积盘密度降低、结构不明显，且有效抑制了非磁化情况下识别的俘获g模。

Conclusion: 研究验证了偏心波形模型SEOBNRv5EHM在潮汐效应不显著时对黑洞-中子星系统的适用性，确认了偏心率对遗迹黑洞性质影响较小但对盘质量有一定影响，并发现强磁场会有效抑制吸积盘中的俘获g模振荡。这些结果为识别黑洞-中子星系统的动力学形成起源和理解并合后遗迹的物理特性提供了重要依据。

Abstract: A confident detection of orbital eccentricity in gravitational-wave signals from black hole-neutron star systems would be a strong indicator of their dynamical formation origin. While eccentric waveform models have recently been developed, their validity for analyzing black hole--neutron star signals has yet to be tested against numerical-relativity simulations. Building upon our previous study of quasicircular black hole--neutron star mergers, we perform simulations of a subset of these systems with orbital eccentricities around five times larger than those inferred for GW200105_162426. Additionally, we perform simulations with magnetized matter to assess whether trapped $g$-mode oscillations persist in strongly magnetized disks. Regarding the emitted gravitational-wave signal, we find that the effective one-body waveform model SEOBNRv5EHM shows reasonable agreement unless tidal contributions become important, and the inspiral-only post-Newtonian models pyEFPE/pyEFPEHM show larger disagreement in the late inspiral. For the equal-mass system, which has the largest effective tidal deformability, eccentric tidal corrections have a small but noticeable impact on the phase, $\sim0.1$ rad. We find an almost negligible impact of eccentricity on the properties of the remnant black hole, and differences of up to a factor of two in the remnant disk mass. Trapped $g$ modes in the accretion disks have the same frequency regardless of the eccentricity, confirming that they depend mainly on the properties of the remnant black hole. We find that when accretion disks are strongly magnetized, they become less dense and have less pronounced structure, and that the trapped $g$ modes previously identified in the unmagnetized cases are efficiently damped.

</details>


### [106] [From Cosmic Bounces to Primordial Black Holes: The Role of Primordial Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21658)
*Ajeet Singhal,Shreya Banerjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非奇异ekpyrotic反弹期间原初磁场产生的原初黑洞(PBH)形成机制。通过保留有限波数截断，计算了曲率谱、密度扰动方差和PBH丰度，发现关键坍缩质量谱峰值约为2.0×10⁻³倍太阳质量，PBH暗物质占比约10⁻⁵，形成次主导的亚太阳质量PBH种群。宇宙学过闭合约束限制了背景参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论框架中，原初黑洞的形成机制与早期宇宙磁场生成存在关联。非奇异ekpyrotic反弹模型提供了一个产生原初磁场的场景，但其与PBH丰度的定量关系尚未明确建立。需要研究时间依赖的电磁场-标量场耦合如何打破共形不变性、放大规范场涨落，以及由此产生的各向异性应力如何主导辐射为主时期并最终形成PBH，同时确定相关宇宙学参数的约束条件。

Method: 1. 采用非奇异ekpyrotic反弹模型，引入电磁场与标量背景场之间的时间依赖耦合机制；2. 通过打破共形不变性，计算收缩期间规范场涨落的放大过程；3. 保留有限波数截断（而非无限截断），计算紫外主导的曲率功率谱；4. 评估密度扰动方差；5. 分别使用单色近似和临界坍缩处方计算PBH丰度和质量谱；6. 应用宇宙学过闭合约束条件（f_PBH ≤ 1）反推收缩末期哈勃尺度和收缩指数等背景参数的允许范围；7. 分析电磁反作用极限下的物理边界一致性。

Result: 对于基准参数，临界坍缩质量谱在M≈2.0×10⁻³M⊙处达到峰值，总PBH暗物质占比f_PBH≈10⁻⁵，形成次主导的亚太阳质量PBH种群。宇宙学过闭合约束（f_PBH ≤ 1）限制了关键背景参数：收缩末期哈勃尺度H_end ≤ 1.6×10³ GeV，收缩指数q ≤ 0.247，这些约束与电磁反作用极限保持物理一致性。研究表明，要获得小行星质量级别的PBH或更大的暗物质占比，需要调整紫外截断和收缩持续时间参数。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了反弹磁生成与PBH丰度预测之间的定量联系，强调了有限波数截断和坍缩处方假设在理论预测中的关键作用。非奇异ekpyrotic反弹产生的原初磁场可以形成次主导的亚太阳质量PBH种群，但其对暗物质的贡献较小（~10⁻⁵）。宇宙学约束为早期宇宙收缩相的物理参数提供了明确边界，为进一步探索反弹宇宙学与原初黑洞形成的关系提供了理论基础。模型参数的调整可能产生不同质量范围和丰度的PBH种群。

Abstract: We investigate primordial black hole (PBH) formation from primordial magnetic fields generated during a non-singular ekpyrotic bounce. A time-dependent coupling between the electromagnetic field and a scalar background breaks conformal invariance and amplifies gauge field fluctuations during contraction, generating magnetic fields whose anisotropic stress dominates during radiation domination. By retaining finite-wavenumber cutoffs, we compute the ultraviolet-dominated curvature spectrum, evaluate density-perturbation variance, and determine PBH abundances using both monochromatic and critical-collapse prescriptions. For benchmark parameters, the critical collapse mass spectrum peaks at $M\simeq 2.0\times10^{-3}M_\odot$ , with a total PBH dark matter fraction of $f_{PBH}\approx 10^{-5}$, forming a subdominant subsolar PBH population. Enforcing the cosmological overclosure bound ($f_{\mathrm{PBH}} \le 1$) constrains the primary background parameters, namely the end-of-contraction Hubble scale ($H_{\mathrm{end}} \le 1.6 \times 10^3~\mathrm{GeV}$) and the contraction exponent ($q \le 0.247$), establishing a physical boundary consistent with the electromagnetic backreaction limit. Shifting to asteroid-mass PBHs or a larger dark matter fraction requires modifying the ultraviolet cutoff and contraction duration. This analysis highlights the essential role of finite-wavenumber cutoffs and assumed-collapse prescriptions in linking bounce magnetogenesis to the predicted PBH abundance.

</details>


### [107] [An exact Simpson-Visser family in bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21701)
*A. A. Araújo Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在度规大黄蜂引力框架下构造了精确的Simpson-Visser解，该解由幻影标量场、磁非线性电动力学和类空固定范数配置支持。研究发现该解包含虫洞、黑洞-反弹等几何结构的连续族，并分析了其光学性质、引力波探测特征及稳定性，发现在特定参数下电磁场会出现不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论（特别是大黄蜂引力）中构造具有物理意义的精确黑洞或虫洞解，并研究洛伦兹对称性破缺对时空几何、观测效应（如阴影、光线偏折）以及场扰动稳定性的影响。现有研究缺乏在大黄蜂引力中包含非线性电动力学和标量场支持的完整解析解及其稳定性分析。

Method: 1) 在度规大黄蜂引力理论中求解场方程，利用幻影标量场、磁非线性电动力学源和类空大黄蜂场配置构造Simpson-Visser型精确解；2) 通过独立矢量方程确定局部拉格朗日乘子和时间分量，确保度规视界处的正则入射延拓；3) 重构物质函数以识别虫洞、黑洞-反弹等几何结构族；4) 计算光子球、临界轨道、光线偏折、近日点进动及阴影等观测量；5) 利用Chebyshev谱配置方法求解轴向耦合方程，计算无质量标量探测场和麦克斯韦探测场的准正规模共振；6) 通过几何光学近似估算张量和大黄蜂扰动的共振尺度；7) 分析探测势的符号以排除指数增长模式；8) 利用变分方法构造增长模及其增长率下界，研究电磁场非常偏振的角向梯度不稳定性。

Result: 1) 成功构造了包含虫洞、零最小几何和黑洞-反弹的连续几何族；2) 在黑洞情形下，面积坐标定义的光子球和临界类时轨道半径保持施瓦西值，但角偏折、近日点进动和光子轨道不稳定性依赖于反弹参数和大黄蜂参数；3) 在固定失效质量和面积观测者位置时，度规阴影与施瓦西预言一致，Sgr A*阴影直径和M87*角质量的观测比较无法单独确定这些参数；4) 轴向耦合方程产生不同的张量、度规和横向大黄蜂特征；5) 通过Chebyshev谱方法计算出辅助无质量标量和普通麦克斯韦探测共振的基频；6) 非负探测势排除了指数增长的探测模式；7) 对于负非最小耦合和非零时间分量情形，支持电磁场在其非常偏振中出现角向梯度不稳定性，该不稳定特征在耦合主符号中持续存在，变分构造确立了固定几何上的增长模及其增长率下界。

Conclusion: 本文在度规大黄蜂引力中成功构造了由幻影标量场和非线性电动力学支持的精确Simpson-Visser解，该解统一描述了虫洞、黑洞-反弹等多种几何结构。虽然某些观测量（如阴影）与施瓦西黑洞一致，但引力偏折和轨道特性对修正参数敏感。稳定性分析揭示了在特定参数区域存在电磁场的角向梯度不稳定性，这对大黄蜂引力模型的物理可行性提出了约束。该研究为在修正引力框架下理解洛伦兹破缺效应及其观测特征提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We construct an exact Simpson-Visser solution in metric bumblebee gravity, supported by a phantom scalar, magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics, and a spacelike configuration of fixed norm. The independent vector equation determines a local Lagrange multiplier and a temporal component that permits a regular ingoing extension across metric horizons. Reconstruction of the matter functions identifies a continuous family of wormholes, null minimum geometries, and black-bounces within one action. In the black hole case, the areal photon sphere and critical timelike orbit radii retain their Schwarzschild values, whereas angular deflection, periapsis advance, and photon orbit instability depend on the bounce and bumblebee parameters. The metric shadow coincides with the Schwarzschild prediction at fixed lapse mass and areal observer position; in addition, the adopted Sgr A* shadow diameter and M87* angular mass comparisons do not determine these parameters separately. The coupled axial equations yield distinct tensor, metric, and transverse bumblebee characteristics. We compute fundamental auxiliary massless scalar and ordinary Maxwell probe resonances by Chebyshev spectral collocation and estimate the tensor and bumblebee resonance scales at leading geometric optics order. Nonnegative probe potentials exclude exponentially growing probe modes. For negative nonminimal coupling and a nonzero temporal case, however, the supporting electromagnetic field develops an angular gradient instability in its extraordinary polarization. The unstable characteristic persists in the coupled principal symbol, while a variational construction establishes growing modes on the fixed geometry and lower bounds on their growth rates.

</details>


### [108] [Thermodynamics of rotating black holes with constrained primary hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21830)
*Wentao Liu,Di Wu,Shao-Wen Wei,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义Proca理论框架下构造了一个渐近平坦的非Kerr旋转黑洞解，其视界在二阶旋转近似下严格满足Killing和等温条件，并建立了包含对数修正的热力学第一定律和修正的Smarr关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当黑洞视界上的表面引力不均匀时，精确的旋转几何不一定对应平衡态黑洞。现有理论需要在修正引力框架下构造满足严格平衡条件（Killing视界和等温条件）的旋转黑洞解，并建立其自洽的热力学体系

Method: 1) 从广义Proca理论的排列零解（aligned null solutions）中构造渐近平坦的非Kerr子族；2) 通过要求外视界在二阶旋转近似下严格满足Killing条件和等温条件来约束矢量毛发；3) 利用归一化视界荷的可积性固定二阶振幅参数；4) 计算完整的引力和非极小耦合矢量表面荷，导出渐近归一化质量和总角动量；5) 推导对数形式的Hamiltonian熵，验证热力学第一定律在双参数解空间两个独立方向上均成立；6) 建立包含有量纲耦合常数的标度修正Smarr关系

Result: 成功构造了一个双参数的渐近平坦非Kerr旋转黑洞解族，其视界在二阶旋转近似下满足严格的平衡条件。获得了包含对数修正项的Hamiltonian熵表达式，验证了热力学第一定律 dM = TdS + ΩdJ 在解空间中成立。导出了质量作为熵和角动量的函数 M(S,J)，恢复了几何热力学势，并建立了保留有量纲耦合常数的修正Smarr关系。精确度量描述完整几何，而平衡热力学自洽地限制在二阶自旋近似

Conclusion: 在广义Proca理论中成功构造了满足严格平衡条件的旋转黑洞解，证明了即使在修正引力理论中，通过适当约束矢量毛发，也能建立自洽的黑洞热力学体系。对数熵修正和标度修正的Smarr关系反映了矢量场耦合对黑洞热力学的本质影响。该工作为研究修正引力理论中的旋转黑洞平衡态和热力学性质提供了严格的理论框架

Abstract: An exact rotating geometry need not define a black hole in equilibrium when its surface gravity varies across the horizon. We construct an asymptotically flat, non-Kerr subfamily of the aligned null solutions of generalized Proca theory whose outer horizon is exactly Killing and isothermal to second order in rotation. The horizon conditions constrain the vector hair, and integrability of the normalized horizon charge fixes the remaining second-order amplitude. Computing the complete gravitational and nonminimal vector surface charges, we obtain the asymptotically normalized mass and total angular momentum, and derive a logarithmic Hamiltonian entropy. The first law holds in both independent directions of the resulting two-parameter solution space. We express the mass as a function of entropy and angular momentum, recover the geometric thermodynamic potentials, and establish a scale-corrected Smarr relation that retains the dimensionful coupling. The exact metric defines the geometry, while equilibrium thermodynamics is consistently restricted to second order in spin.

</details>


### [109] [Photon-Return and Polar-Focusing Crossovers near Extremal Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21848)
*Valentina Gonzalez-Moya,Ali Ovgun,Joel Saavedra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了近极端Kerr黑洞光子绕行的精确标度律：当光子完成n次绕行时，冲击参数的调谐精度遵循εn=√3κ/(e^{2πX}-1)+O(κ²|lnκ|)，其中κ=√(1-a²/M²)表征黑洞与极端态的偏离程度。研究还发现极向Jacobi相位的第二标度律，证明了近极端Kerr黑洞的绕行尺度和极向聚焦尺度存在参数化分离，而非极端态与亚极端态之间的不连续跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端Kerr黑洞的引力透镜效应表现出特殊的绕行层级结构，但从极端态到亚极端态过渡时的标度行为尚不清楚。需要理解：(1)光子完成多次绕行所需的冲击参数精度如何依赖于近极端参数κ；(2)相邻光线的聚焦行为如何随绕行次数演化；(3)极端态和亚极端态的透镜层级之间是否存在平滑过渡还是不连续跳变。这些问题对理解强引力场中的光传播和黑洞阴影结构至关重要。

Method: 采用解析和数值相结合的方法：(1)针对赤道面顺行返回轨道，通过渐近分析推导冲击参数εn与绕行次数n、近极端参数κ的标度关系；(2)引入无量纲参数X=nκ统一描述极端和亚极端情形；(3)对准赤道扰动情况，分析极向运动的Jacobi相位Ψθ，推导其关于X的第二标度律；(4)研究聚焦点在绕行数空间的分布从几何间隔到渐近均匀间隔的转变；(5)通过精确Kerr度规的径向动力学数值积分和线性极向方程求解验证解析标度律。

Result: 获得两个关键标度律：(1)绕行冲击参数标度律εn=√3κ/(e^{2πX}-1)+O(κ²|lnκ|)，该单一公式连接了极端态的反绕行层级(X→0)与亚极端态的指数累积(X≫1)；(2)极向Jacobi相位标度律Ψθ=2ln[sinh(πX)/κ]+Cs+o(1)。聚焦点间隔从绕行数的几何分布过渡到渐近均匀分布ΔN≃(2κ)^{-1}。数值模拟完全验证了这两个标度崩缩(scaling collapse)，不同κ和n值的数据在标度变量X下塌缩到统一曲线上。

Conclusion: 近极端Kerr黑洞表现出参数化可分离的绕行尺度和极向聚焦尺度，而非极端态与亚极端态透镜层级之间的不连续跳变。单一标度变量X=nκ能够统一描述从极端到亚极端的完整过渡区域。这表明强引力透镜效应在近极端区域具有普适的标度结构，为理解快速旋转黑洞的光学特性和观测特征提供了理论基础。该结果对黑洞阴影精密观测和极端Kerr时空的稳定性分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: How precisely must a photon's impact parameter be tuned for it to complete $n$ windings around a nearly extremal Kerr black hole before escaping, and how does the same near extremal scaling control the focusing of neighboring rays? For equatorial prograde returns we find, with $κ=\sqrt{1-a^2/M^2}$ and $X=nκ$, $ε_n=\sqrt{3}\,κ/(e^{2πX}-1) +O(κ^2|\lnκ|)$. This single law connects the extremal inverse-winding hierarchy to subextremal exponential accumulation. Perturbing the return family quasi-equatorially, we derive a second scaling law for the polar Jacobi phase, $Ψ_θ=2\ln[\sinh(πX)/κ]+C_s+o(1)$, and show that its focusing points cross from geometric spacing in winding number to asymptotically uniform spacing $Δ\mathcal N\simeq(2κ)^{-1}$. Numerical integrations of the exact Kerr radial dynamics and linear polar equation verify both scaling collapses. Thus near extremal Kerr exhibits parametrically distinct winding and polar-focusing scales, rather than a discontinuous jump between extremal and subextremal lensing hierarchies.

</details>


### [110] [From Accelerating Rotating Black Holes to a Swirling Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21860)
*José Barrientos,Adolfo Cisterna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过对Kerr C-metric施加协同的视界退化和标度程序,成功恢复了旋转宇宙(swirling universe)几何。该方法利用加速旋转黑洞的视界结构,使内外视界重合并同时趋近加速视界,最终将黑洞对推至无穷远处,但保留有限的引力磁扭曲,从而给出旋转宇宙的物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转宇宙(swirling universe)的几何结构缺乏清晰的物理起源解释。为了理解其内禀旋转的物理本质,需要找到一种从已知黑洞时空(如Kerr C-metric)通过系统的极限过程构造旋转宇宙的方法,从而揭示其内禀旋转与旋转黑洞引力效应之间的联系。

Method: 采用协同的视界退化(horizon degeneration)和标度(scaling)程序作用于Kerr C-metric。具体方法包括:(1)利用加速旋转黑洞的视界结构,使其内视界和外视界合并;(2)同时让黑洞视界趋近加速视界;(3)以快于黑洞视界趋近速度的标度放大加速视界附近区域;(4)在此极限过程中,加速旋转黑洞对被推至无穷远的固有距离,但引力磁扭曲(gravitomagnetic twist)保持有限。

Result: 通过上述协同极限程序,成功从Kerr C-metric精确地重构出旋转宇宙几何。结果表明,旋转宇宙中保留的有限引力磁扭曲正是被推至无穷远的旋转黑洞对留下的引力学遗迹。该构造为旋转宇宙的内禀旋转提供了明确的物理解释。

Conclusion: 本研究证明旋转宇宙可以通过系统的极限程序从Kerr C-metric导出,其内禀旋转具有清晰的物理起源:它是被送至无穷远的旋转黑洞的引力学残余。这一结果建立了加速旋转黑洞时空与旋转宇宙几何之间的直接联系,为理解具有整体旋转的宇宙学模型提供了新的物理图景。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the swirling universe can be recovered through a coordinated horizon-degeneration and scaling procedure applied to the Kerr C-metric. The construction exploits the horizon structure of accelerating, rotating black holes, which allows their inner and outer horizons to coalesce while simultaneously approaching the acceleration horizon. By magnifying the near-acceleration-horizon region faster than the black hole horizons approach it, the pair of accelerating, rotating black holes is pushed to an infinite proper distance, while a finite gravitomagnetic twist survives. The resulting geometry reproduces precisely the swirling universe, providing a physical interpretation of its intrinsic rotation as the gravitational remnant of rotating black holes sent to infinity.

</details>


### [111] [Breakdown of hydrodynamic universality in causal first-order viscous neutron star oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21966)
*Lennox S. Keeble,Alejandro Cárdenas-Avendaño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了高粘性中子星流体动力学建模中的"流体动力学普适性"破缺问题。研究表明，在BDNK理论框架下，当体积粘度达到ζ~10³⁰-10³¹ g/cm/s范围时，因果性和有限尺寸效应导致普适性失效，振荡模式结构发生实质性改变，这为一阶相对论流体动力学理论可能不足以描述高粘性中子星提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论流体动力学的不同表述形式在长波长极限下应包含相同的物理内容，这被称为"流体动力学普适性"。然而，最近研究发现在径向中子星振荡中，这种普适性可能在高粘性星体中因果律和有限尺寸效应而破缺。本研究旨在系统探究这种普适性破缺在BDNK（Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun）理论的因果流体动力学框架中的普遍性，以及对中子星振荡模式和物理稳定性的影响。

Method: 研究采用BDNK相对论流体动力学理论框架，系统分析了广泛范围内的因果流体动力学参考系（causal hydrodynamic frames）。通过考察不同体积粘度条件下中子星的径向振荡行为，研究了粘度增加对各类因果参考系的影响，特别关注了这些参考系何时超出BDNK理论的有效性范围。同时分析了振荡模式结构的变化，包括非流体动力学模式的阻尼时间尺度、基本模式与泛音模式的寿命比较，以及潜在不稳定性的出现。此外，还检验了无粘引力坍缩阈值在不同情况下的变化。

Result: 研究发现，随着粘度增加，广泛范围的因果参考系被集体推离BDNK理论的有效性区域。当体积粘度达到ζ~10³⁰-10³¹ g/cm/s范围时，所有考察的因果参考系都位于BDNK有效区域之外。普适性破缺伴随着振荡模式结构的实质性改变：非流体动力学模式的阻尼时间尺度变得与流体动力学模式相当；第一泛音模式的寿命可能超过基本模式；出现了亚毫秒时间尺度的非物理不稳定性。值得注意的是，即使在普适性破缺的区域，无粘引力坍缩阈值在BDNK理论中仍保持不变。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了重要证据表明，一阶相对论流体动力学理论可能不足以准确建模高粘性中子星。在足够高的粘度条件下（ζ~10³⁰-10³¹ g/cm/s），流体动力学普适性在BDNK理论的因果参考系中普遍破缺，导致振荡模式结构发生根本性变化并可能出现非物理不稳定性。这些发现对中子星物理建模具有重要意义，提示需要考虑更高阶的流体动力学理论或其他理论框架来准确描述高粘性中子星的动力学行为。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic modeling of viscous neutron stars requires a choice of theory of relativistic hydrodynamics. That distinct formulations contain the same physical content in the long-wavelength limit is referred to as ``hydrodynamic universality.'' It has been recently shown that hydrodynamic universality can break down in radial neutron star oscillations: causality and finite-size effects restrict access to the long-wavelength universal hydrodynamic regime in sufficiently viscous stars. We show that this breakdown occurs for a broad range of causal hydrodynamic frames in Bemfica--Disconzi--Noronha--Kovtun (BDNK) theory. Increasing viscosity collectively pushes this class of causal frames away from the BDNK regime of validity, eventually forcing all to lie outside it at bulk viscosities in the range $ζ\sim10^{30}$--$10^{31}\mathrm{g}/\mathrm{cm}/\mathrm{s}$. This breakdown of universality is accompanied by material changes in the oscillation mode structure: non-hydrodynamic modes become damped on timescales comparable to those of hydrodynamic modes, the first overtone can become longer lived than the fundamental mode, and unphysical instabilities with sub-millisecond timescales can emerge. Even in regimes where universality has broken down, we find that the inviscid threshold of gravitational collapse remains unchanged in BDNK theory. These results provide evidence that first-order theories of relativistic hydrodynamics may be insufficient for modeling sufficiently viscous neutron stars.

</details>


### [112] [Exact algebraic separation of the gravitoelectromagnetic perturbations of Kerr-Newman-(anti-)de Sitter black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22274)
*Jun-Kun Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对带电旋转的Kerr-Newman-(反)de Sitter黑洞，实现了引力-电磁耦合辐射方程的显式分离，构造了具有常数行列式的变换矩阵，统一处理正负自旋扇区，并在整个外部区域保持光滑可逆性，为研究准正则模、黑洞稳定性及全息应用奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非极端Kerr-Newman-(反)de Sitter黑洞时空中，引力场和电磁场的辐射方程呈现耦合状态，难以直接求解和分析。为了研究这类带电旋转黑洞的准正则模式、稳定性问题以及探索全息对偶应用，需要发展一种有效的数学方法将耦合的辐射方程显式分离，并确保该方法在整个相关时空区域（包括事件视界和宇宙学视界之间）保持数学上的良好性质。

Method: 构造了一个行列式为常数的变换矩阵，该矩阵的选择独立于频率和方位角量子数，能够在同一框架内统一处理正自旋和负自旋扇区。通过这一变换矩阵实现了耦合方程的分离，得到独立的径向和角向方程组，这些方程在任意复频率下均有效。证明了该变换在开放的反de Sitter外部区域以及de Sitter时空中事件视界与宇宙学视界之间区域的光滑性和可逆性。将辐射场的Newman-Penrose变量在旋转轴和未来视界处的正则性条件表达为分离解应满足的条件。在渐近平坦极限下，将所构造的变换与Hintz的矩阵积分因子通过允许的标架自由度建立了联系。

Result: 成功获得了非极端Kerr-Newman-(反)de Sitter黑洞上引力-电磁耦合辐射方程的显式分离形式。所得的径向和角向分离方程在任意复频率下有效，变换矩阵在相关时空区域内保持光滑可逆。明确建立了辐射场在物理边界（旋转轴、未来视界）处的正则性条件与分离解之间的对应关系。验证了在渐近平坦极限下，新方法与已有的Hintz矩阵积分因子方法通过标架变换相关联，确保了理论的自洽性。

Conclusion: 本研究为带电旋转Kerr-Newman-(反)de Sitter黑洞的引力-电磁辐射问题提供了完整的数学框架。所构造的分离变换具有良好的数学性质（常数行列式、光滑可逆、频率独立），为后续研究这类黑洞的准正则模谱、动力学稳定性以及AdS/CFT对应框架下的全息应用提供了坚实的理论基础。该方法统一处理了不同自旋扇区，并与渐近平坦情形下的已知结果相容，展现了广泛的适用性和理论价值。

Abstract: We obtain an explicit separation of the coupled gravitational and electromagnetic radiative equations on nonextremal Kerr--Newman--(anti-)de Sitter black holes with nonzero charge and rotation. The construction uses a matrix with constant determinant, chosen independently of the frequency and azimuthal number, and treats the positive- and negative-spin sectors within the same formulation. We establish that this transformation is smooth and invertible throughout the open AdS exterior and between the event and cosmological horizons in dS. The resulting radial and angular systems are valid at arbitrary complex frequencies. We also express regularity of the radiative Newman--Penrose variables at the rotation axes and future horizons as conditions on the separated solutions. In the asymptotically flat limit, the transformation is related to Hintz's matrix integrating factor by the allowed frame freedom. These results provide a basis for studying quasinormal modes and black-hole stability in Kerr--Newman--(anti-)de Sitter spacetimes, as well as exploring holographic applications to strongly coupled systems.

</details>
